# Vetus Testamentum — JSTOR Dump Archive

_Generated: 2026-08-24 from 2532 raw dump rows → 2513 articles (deduped by `https://www.jstor.org/stable/...`), plus 6 front-matter rows and ~13 review-like titles. Captures everything needed to avoid re-fetching JSTOR/Brill._

**Sources merged (deduplicated by stable URL):**
- `/tmp/jstor_dump.txt` (53 rows, Vol 72 Fasc 4/5 latest, `48805258-48805272`)
- `/tmp/jstor_72_latest.txt` (33 rows, Vol 72 Fasc 1 `48665415-48665425` + Fasc 3 `48681234-48681240`)
- `/tmp/jstor_12_new.txt` (12 rows, `1518927` `1519466` `20503924` `20504393` `20700054` `41308991` `43894071` `43894175` `43894314` `43894623` `44647542` `26566775`)
- `/tmp/jstor_50_54_new.txt` (774 rows, Vol 50-54 parsed)
- `/tmp/59_dump.txt` (571 rows, Vol 59 Fasc 4 `20700014-20700021` etc.)
- `/tmp/big_dump_raw.txt` (140 rows, `e26842721` `26842722` etc.)
- `/tmp/filtered_map.json` / `/tmp/combined_dump_map.json` (1184 JSTOR stables deduped, canonical title map)
- Crossref via `/tmp/vt_all.json` (ISSN `0042-4935`, `from-pub-date:2000`, 1432 works) for Vol/Fasc/Year/DOI/Pages fill
- Current catalog `data/external/vetus-testamentum-articles.json` (295 verse-linked resources, 2026-08-24 → 261 JSTOR stables + 34 search fallbacks awaiting JSTOR ingest Vol 73-76)

**Counts:** 2532 total stables | 2513 articles | 261 currently linked (verse) | 2271 not verse-linked | JSTOR coverage `1951-2022` (Vol 1-72)

**Fields per row:** Title | Author | Pages | Vol | Fasc | Year | JSTOR Stable | Brill DOI | Linked → targets (catalog id) — all data needed to reconstruct `data/external/vetus-testamentum-articles.json` without re-hitting JSTOR (which is Cloudfront bot-protected `/_fs-ch-1T1wmsGaOgGaSxcX` → 401 on `webfetch`/`cur l`/`/action/doBasicSearch`) or Crossref (`https://api.crossref.org/journals/0042-4935/works`). Diplomacy: use `Vol/Fasc/Page` triple when title normalization fails (Hebrew diacritics, `<em>`, roman numerals `V 16` / `CXXII 4` / `1Kön`).

**Linked column:** `✅ linked → targets (id)` means present in `data/external/vetus-testamentum-articles.json` with a `verse:*` target. `— not linked` means article exists on JSTOR but is not verse/lemma-specific (theological, source-critical, etc.) or not yet hand-mapped. Front-matter rows marked `— — excluded`.
